<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:54d6f2a75166020147ffdfc50b5ab401b023296b:feature:streaming-output-filtering repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=54d6f2a75166020147ffdfc50b5ab401b023296b featureId=streaming-output-filtering sourceCommits=54d6f2a75166020147ffdfc50b5ab401b023296b updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:56.040Z -->

<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:8ba7fcb5ea2ad087d0c351020249184e2cb338e5:feature:workflow-guardrail-api-classification repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=8ba7fcb5ea2ad087d0c351020249184e2cb338e5 featureId=workflow-guardrail-api-classification sourceCommits=8ba7fcb5ea2ad087d0c351020249184e2cb338e5 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:56.951Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->

<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:fa9aa03412d66ffa1c9c3ba233cb22b52699403a:feature:tenant-scoped-persistence-schema repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=fa9aa03412d66ffa1c9c3ba233cb22b52699403a featureId=tenant-scoped-persistence-schema sourceCommits=fa9aa03412d66ffa1c9c3ba233cb22b52699403a updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:56.961Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:904ba4be8b0c74bfe263a9d941a412628da82b90:feature:event-history-replay-runtime repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=904ba4be8b0c74bfe263a9d941a412628da82b90 featureId=event-history-replay-runtime sourceCommits=904ba4be8b0c74bfe263a9d941a412628da82b90 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:56.995Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:510f28519f5659a7b3f81cef32a7f40b1c49701a:feature:postgres-run-store-export repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=510f28519f5659a7b3f81cef32a7f40b1c49701a featureId=postgres-run-store-export sourceCommits=510f28519f5659a7b3f81cef32a7f40b1c49701a updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:56.999Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:0aa5f67af6ed39a1ae8a44f752f2287b20b907ca:feature:fail-closed-output-filters repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=0aa5f67af6ed39a1ae8a44f752f2287b20b907ca featureId=fail-closed-output-filters sourceCommits=0aa5f67af6ed39a1ae8a44f752f2287b20b907ca updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.034Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:669c32a0e51fc55f2a4d3aaf0efcc2fcea1678e7:feature:subagent-structured-failure-logging repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=669c32a0e51fc55f2a4d3aaf0efcc2fcea1678e7 featureId=subagent-structured-failure-logging sourceCommits=669c32a0e51fc55f2a4d3aaf0efcc2fcea1678e7 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.037Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:62b875b9e1f4db40b49a8bfc5c9cf760f6f02d33:feature:runtime-error-normalization repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=62b875b9e1f4db40b49a8bfc5c9cf760f6f02d33 featureId=runtime-error-normalization sourceCommits=62b875b9e1f4db40b49a8bfc5c9cf760f6f02d33 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.043Z -->

- **Server Runtime Security and Lifecycle Hardening**: Server runtime hardening: safer production auth defaults, bounded request handling, validated OpenAI-compatible requests, and explicit app lifecycle controls. _Tags: server, security, middleware, runtime, validation, rate-limiting, openai-compat._ ([README.md](README.md))
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:3c51f1ac75517c86076afed454f346d0aee493ca:feature:tenant-scoped-ws-event-bridge repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=3c51f1ac75517c86076afed454f346d0aee493ca featureId=tenant-scoped-ws-event-bridge sourceCommits=3c51f1ac75517c86076afed454f346d0aee493ca updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.049Z -->
- **Tenant-Scoped WS Event Bridge**: fail-closed multi-tenant WebSocket event streaming with server-resolved tenant isolation. _Tags: websocket, multi-tenant, event-bridge, tenant-isolation, access-control._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:5d153600ebaabaef0a618ba2a8ba46438294265c:feature:durable-audit-log-integrity repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=5d153600ebaabaef0a618ba2a8ba46438294265c featureId=durable-audit-log-integrity sourceCommits=5d153600ebaabaef0a618ba2a8ba46438294265c updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.054Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->

- **Capability-Aware Test Gates**: classify test coverage by capability tier and fail required integration lanes when dependencies are missing. _Tags: testing, integration-tests, capability-report, developer-tooling._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:a78b3775ee380d0d0251ad871182eb4ee58b90f0:feature:prompt-injection-tool-result-guardrail repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=a78b3775ee380d0d0251ad871182eb4ee58b90f0 featureId=prompt-injection-tool-result-guardrail sourceCommits=a78b3775ee380d0d0251ad871182eb4ee58b90f0 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.067Z -->
- **Prompt Injection Tool Result Guardrail**: Prompt injection tool-result guardrail: quote and label untrusted tool output before it enters agent message history. _Tags: prompt-injection, guardrails, tool-results, agent-runtime, security._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:9230f57b832e6f652cc454532b881478fff6127a:feature:secure-docker-sandbox-argv-execution repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=9230f57b832e6f652cc454532b881478fff6127a featureId=secure-docker-sandbox-argv-execution sourceCommits=9230f57b832e6f652cc454532b881478fff6127a updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.070Z -->
- **Secure Docker Sandbox Argv Execution**: Secure Docker sandbox execution: run argv commands without shell interpretation and apply stricter container isolation. _Tags: docker, sandbox, codegen, command-execution, security._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:35cddf1f5f4bbc0ddb893bf0906195a5aba9ddb6:feature:agent-adapter-fidelity-guardrails repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=35cddf1f5f4bbc0ddb893bf0906195a5aba9ddb6 featureId=agent-adapter-fidelity-guardrails sourceCommits=35cddf1f5f4bbc0ddb893bf0906195a5aba9ddb6 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.073Z -->
- **Agent Adapter Fidelity Guardrails**: scoped memory recall, system-prompt-aware routing, and preflight execution checks. _Tags: agent-adapters, memory-enrichment, routing, preflight, guardrails._ ([README.md](README.md))
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:ab1a30762755266e72eb9d90c61f7827a5f38d30:feature:capability-aware-model-fallback-tool-audits repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=ab1a30762755266e72eb9d90c61f7827a5f38d30 featureId=capability-aware-model-fallback-tool-audits sourceCommits=ab1a30762755266e72eb9d90c61f7827a5f38d30 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.076Z -->
- **Capability-Aware Fallback And Tool Audits**: select fallback models by required capabilities or context window and emit best-effort per-tool-call audit records. _Tags: llm, fallback, tool-auditing, observability._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:b6c67194a53b6fa960f69ab803582a0265c318a4:feature:tool-call-audit-types repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=b6c67194a53b6fa960f69ab803582a0265c318a4 featureId=tool-call-audit-types sourceCommits=b6c67194a53b6fa960f69ab803582a0265c318a4 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.079Z -->

<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:be08a655ab81e2290428e71d56228cd8f4012938:feature:workflow-runtime-resilience-controls repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=be08a655ab81e2290428e71d56228cd8f4012938 featureId=workflow-runtime-resilience-controls sourceCommits=be08a655ab81e2290428e71d56228cd8f4012938 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.084Z -->
- **Workflow Runtime Resilience Controls**: recover workflow runs from checkpoints and surface stuck workflow detection with structured validation errors. _Tags: workflow, checkpointing, stuck-detection, adapter-errors._ ([docs/PUBLIC_API_SURFACE_ALLOWLISTS.md](docs/PUBLIC_API_SURFACE_ALLOWLISTS.md), [docs/SERVER_API_SURFACE_INDEX.md](docs/SERVER_API_SURFACE_INDEX.md))
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:65762ccc81cd3fa9d666d5dd675eced47167e386:feature:workflow-stuck-detection-checkpoint-recovery repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=65762ccc81cd3fa9d666d5dd675eced47167e386 featureId=workflow-stuck-detection-checkpoint-recovery sourceCommits=65762ccc81cd3fa9d666d5dd675eced47167e386 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.087Z -->
- **Workflow Stuck Detection and Checkpoint Recovery**: compiled workflows can checkpoint after each node, resume crashed runs from saved checkpoints, and notify callers when execution becomes stuck. _Tags: workflow, checkpointing, crash-recovery, stuck-detection._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:feature-group:destructive-command-guard:006f63e49e60 repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=e2cd4faecb72302c84a2a6ea5ffaf0ebd8b05e01 featureId=destructive-command-guard sourceCommits=e2cd4faecb72302c84a2a6ea5ffaf0ebd8b05e01,0540775317d83b1abc0edb3a80bd3841a7a94d77 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.090Z -->
- **Destructive Command Guard**: broadened detection for root wipe commands and remote shell execution pipes. _Tags: security, agent-adapters, command-guard, shell-safety, shell-commands, destructive-command-guard._
<!-- /workspace-feature:entry -->
<!-- workspace-feature:entry id=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent:2f20d6f3ea615c6feba762a62c611b7a39e8ecd0:feature:custom-shell-tool-destructive-command-guard repo=out/workspace-changelog-remote/full-rebuild-ssh-20260626T0853Z/worktrees/dzupagent commit=2f20d6f3ea615c6feba762a62c611b7a39e8ecd0 featureId=custom-shell-tool-destructive-command-guard sourceCommits=2f20d6f3ea615c6feba762a62c611b7a39e8ecd0 updatedAt=2026-06-27T01:43:57.092Z -->
